# Changelog

All notable changes to this project are documented here. Generated
mechanically from the spec graph (`aida changelog refresh`) — do not edit
by hand; regenerate after merging.

## [v0.16.0] — 2026-10-20

Specs merged since v0.15.0 (961):

### Features

- **STORY-1663** — aida pr ship supports rebase merges with the same gates as squash, so verified slice stacks stop bypassing it (#2732)
- **TASK-1733** — Regen gap classification and headline metric: oracle pass rate is the headline, gaps trended by category (#2667)
- **TASK-1732** — Regen seam-adapter score (R2): score through a name-mapping adapter and report R1, R2 and the delta (#2666)
- **STORY-1654** — Relay/orchestrator reviewers run as separate processes with their own identity, so BUG-1918's same-session self-approval gate does not stop relay merges (#2661)
- **STORY-1661** — Background history indexer: keep the history index current after store commits so bare 'aida history' rarely falls back to the digest (#2601)
- **TASK-1738** — aida-handoff Step 1 should run /aida-capture (incl. FAQ lens), not sweep mentally (#2589)
- **TASK-1736** — Warn before a seat grant expires; relay refuses to launch a seat whose grant has under 1h left (#2588)
- **TASK-1538** — Slice 1: mechanical split of lib.rs into ~45 flat modules (STORY-1488 T2) (#2527, #2530, #2551, #2554)
- **STORY-1680** — Flag in-progress specs nothing is working in aida awaiting after an idle threshold (STORY-1653 slice B) (#2553)
- **STORY-1653** — Show who is driving each in-progress spec (relay, drain, agent, queued, or idle) so live work is distinguishable from abandoned work (#2542)
- **STORY-1652** — Render markdown in aida show's human output (headings, emphasis, code, lists, aligned tables) instead of printing raw markdown (#2540)
- **STORY-1656** — aida init creates the repo on every configured forge in one command (GitHub + GitLab group), creating GitLab projects via the API when push-to-create is refused (#2536)
- **STORY-1647** — Pre-commit refuses commits in the primary checkout on main; agents commit only in worktrees (#2531)
- **STORY-1648** — Flag primary-checkout drift at session start and in the status line (#2523)
- **STORY-1645** — aida faq topics: list FAQ topic tags with counts (#2522)
- **STORY-1641** — Git-style external subcommand dispatch: unrecognised aida <name> runs aida-<name> from PATH (#2520)
- **TASK-1717** — Serialize ordinary store sync, direct merge-gate and canonical mailbox with the existing writer lock (#2502)
- **STORY-1649** — Agents can't see where a spec shipped, and nothing checks it when a spec is completed: show git linkage in TOON aida show and warn when a code spec is completed with no shipped commit (#2501)
- **STORY-1650** — aida history defaults to the status flow (one line per status change); the per-spec digest moves to --digest (#2491)
- **STORY-1644** — aida list: default sort by natural numeric ID (FAQ-2 before FAQ-11); configurable sort and agent row cap (30 → 100) with explicit truncation notice (#2490)
- **STORY-1643** — /aida-capture should also capture FAQ candidates (question + verified answer), following the FAQ-11 convention (#2486)
- **STORY-1642** — Knowledge types (faq, decision, vision, term, principle) are reference, not work: hide from default list and never pickable (#2484)
- **STORY-1540** — aida init at a human TTY seeds the initializer into the new store's team roster (#2476)
- **TASK-1720** — feat(dev): add aida dev rebuild subcommand (#2471)
- **TASK-1718** — add --path flag to print the current binary execution path (#2469)
- **TASK-1330** — Identity hygiene gate: refuse pushes/commits with non-allowlisted author emails (#2449)
- **TASK-1596** — Register monthly merged-worktrees guard + advisor route in the schedule registry (#2430)
- **TASK-1592** — File-backed session grants for role enter (#2421)
- **TASK-1590** — Add aida review list command to show statuses of all review verdicts (#2419)
- **TASK-1578** — Role-enter picker: stop parsing an in-project session log once its resolved role is rejected (BUG-1789 follow-up) (#2416)
- **TASK-1583** — Add aida list pr command to view open PRs with mapped specs (#2406)
- **TASK-1569** — aida show: print comment bodies by default on a human TTY, keep count + hint in agent output (#2395)
- **TASK-1577** — Cleanup target/worktrees/<name> upon session end (#2392)
- **STORY-1480** — Close cycle-time instrumentation gaps and publish per-spec timing records across machines (#2367)
- **STORY-1479** — Aggregate cycle-time breakdown: aida metrics cycle-time (per-activity p50/p90, work vs wait, rework rates, slowest specs) (#2366)
- **TASK-1527** — Durable cache refresh request and detached refresh worker (slice C) (#2349)
- **TASK-1562** — Disk-headroom guard advises two commands that cannot reclaim the space; no command reclaims stale worktree target caches (#2331)
- **TASK-1555** — Give agents one local entrypoint for the CI-only gates (make check-ci) (#2330)
- **TASK-1564** — Record a drain phase failure as a comment on the parent spec, not as its own draft spec (#2329)
- **TASK-1536** — Scaffold refresh footprint follow-ups: gate the minimal footprint, flag already-flooded lane projects, detect pre-footprint lanes by AGENTS.md heading (#2327)
- **TASK-1526** — Single-flight cache refresh and stale labels (slice B) (#2283)
- **TASK-1500** — Offer configurable YOLO-mode prompt during aida init (#2259)
- **STORY-1476** — Require trusted-config opt-in before graded review runs spec-authored acceptance commands (#2217)
- **TASK-1462** — aida agent new: honor active role in picker and label description optional (#2154)
- **STORY-1419** — Nothing tells a reviewer that the rework they are blocking has landed - the head advancing past the verdict's reviewed_sha is computable and no surface reports it (#2047)
- **STORY-1391** — A finding that survives a review round is evidence the brief is unimplementable, and nothing can detect it because verdict files are overwritten each round (#2045)
- **TASK-1300** — Mechanically enumerate external-prose classifiers, because three review rounds proved the workspace-wide audit cannot converge by hand (#2044)
- **STORY-1041** — Pipeline depth state contract: [drain] pipeline_depth config + drain-state persistence + status rendering (foundation for the concurrent scheduler) (#1785, #2041)
- **TASK-1293** — Post-deployment acceptance criteria belong on a follow-up spec, never on the spec that ships the change — a fifth failure class, found deadlocking TASK-1291 (#2003)
- **STORY-1352** — Declare and guard a versioned read-only monitor contract (the eleven --json surfaces + an events follow feed) so an external dashboard cannot break silently (#1987)
- **TASK-1279** — Seat-parity audit: prove Codex can occupy every seat (product, advisor, implementer, reviewer, integrator) interactively and headless; fix the Claude-only surfaces (#1984)
- **TASK-1291** — Round-1 reviewer does a full sweep: enumerate every acceptance criterion with its verification status and list all defects, so rounds converge instead of tailing one finding at a time (#1971)
- **TASK-1278** — Typed protocols slice 3: lane layer (research, docs, keystone) + MCP resource aida://protocol/<type> + headless implementer/reviewer prompt injection (#1951)
- **TASK-1276** — Spikes are drain candidates when the advisor sets execution_mode = drain: mode overrides the type's human-only default and routes through the research-lane contract (#1947)
- **TASK-1271** — Mailbox latency is unobservable: expose oldest-unread age + last-seen time per recipient (aida mailbox latency / in aida awaiting --notice and statusbar) so a seat can prove it is not remiss in readin (#1942)
- **TASK-1268** — queue progress --batch and next[] hints name the --single-branch cluster command when the batch carries serialize verdicts (#1931)
- **TASK-1266** — Groom proposes lanes: serialize verdicts print the --single-branch cluster command; docs-only/papercut specs get a lifecycle:trivial proposal (#1929)
- **TASK-1258** — Multi-language test scanning (v1 scans Rust `#[test]` fns) (#1906)
- **TASK-1249** — [HITL] Slice 4: wire the harvest gate into the EPIC-66 drain pipeline as an advisory Phase::Harvest (#1897)
- **TASK-1248** — [HITL] Slice 3: aida reconstitute <spec> — the store-only reconstitution probe (regenerate tests, diff, score, feed harvest) (#1895)
- **STORY-1166** — GitLab Slice 3: abstract CI-watch + delegated-review check-runs behind the Forge trait (#1894)
- **TASK-161** — Stale merge-hold markers after orchestrated merge + no user-facing merge-hold clear command (#1875)
- **TASK-1243** — Merge-lease: wire the lease across pr ship's check→merge→pull (drain phase split to follow-up) (#1874)
- **STORY-1171** — Branch merge-serialization lock: one agent merges to a target branch at a time (coordination primitive) (#1873)
- **TASK-1239** — aida agent new picker: offer stakeholder personas (guest/requester) in a separate labeled sub-section (#1859)
- **STORY-1155** — v1: selector-scoped agent-review gate inserted before merge (reuses the reviewer/verdict/shelve machinery) (#1855)
- **STORY-1158** — v1: aida why <spec> --plain — generate + cache a separate plain-English rationale + example layer (#1854)
- **TASK-1236** — aida agent role picker: annotate live-driver / single-instance status BEFORE selection (don't select-then-error) (#1849)
- **ADR-34** — Driver seats may have non-authoritative companion instances (#1848)
- **STORY-1133** — Companion (non-driver) instances of a singleton driver role: converse + read + draft, but never dispose/drive/hold the seat (#1848)
- **STORY-1131** — aida config menu becomes an EDIT surface (not read-only), starting with the permission posture + AIDA-MCP-registration toggle (#1846)
- **STORY-1129** — Make the token-efficient CLI the DEFAULT agent surface end-to-end for CLI-capable agents; MCP stays the opt-in typed surface for MCP-native clients (#1844)
- **STORY-1125** — Queueable release meta-task: readiness + prep + stop-for-confirm, never headless (#1841)
- **STORY-1128** — aida config permissions set contained|native|bypass [--user|--local] + doctor heal: idempotent TOML-merge writer for the posture files (#1839)
- **STORY-1110** — Least-privilege guest/requester role: read surfaces + Draft-only intake, gated out of the build loop (#1838)
- **STORY-1124** — Codex 'contained-autonomous' posture: prompt-free BUT sandboxed (workspace-write + approve-for-me), a middle tier between codex-defaults and --bypass-sandbox (#1837)
- **STORY-1127** — aida config permissions show + doctor --category permission-posture (check): resolve & display the effective posture, flag full-YOLO-no-sandbox and inconsistencies (#1835)
- **TASK-1235** — aida derisk <SPEC> CLI verb: a thin vendor-aware launcher for the /aida-derisk skill (advisor session) (#1834)
- **STORY-1132** — /aida-derisk: climb a spec down the autonomy ladder (guided→drain) by locking its design forks into ADRs+acceptance and mandating the safety gates the drain's CI/review enforce (#1830)
- **TASK-1231** — Robust unattended autonomous-progress: one tested 'safely drain the ready set from anywhere' command, not a fragile crontab one-liner (#1827)
- **TASK-1221** — `/aida-oversee` skill (judgment layer) — after the loop is dogfooded (#1807)
- **STORY-1096** — Formalize the OVERSIGHT seat: a meta-supervisor above drive that keeps the operator out of babysitting AND agent-to-agent relay (#1803)
- **STORY-1094** — Memory-lane reflex scaffolding: query-first + capture-as-you-go agent guidance (CLI/TOON surface, zero machinery vocabulary) (#1799)
- **TASK-1215** — Memory-lane hygiene floor: capture-as-you-go plus a light staleness signal so authored memory does not silently rot (#1793)
- **STORY-1093** — Memory-lane init footprint tier: scaffold store + reflex skills + reflex CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md block, and NOTHING queue/drain/orchestrator/role-related (#1791)
- **STORY-1091** — Concurrent single-spec drain scheduler: run spec B's implementer while spec A waits on CI/review/merge (the STORY-1041 scheduler, beyond the depth-state slice) (#1787)
- **STORY-1051** — Re-drive supervisor: auto-retry TRANSIENT-parked specs on a loop (backoff + cap), leave genuine-needs-human parks for escalation (#1784)
- **STORY-994** — Capture terminal identity at agent launch and title the tab so a human can find the session (#1704, #1775)

- **STORY-1048** — Mirror release tags and publish binaries to GitLab (releases are GitHub-only today) (#1770)
- **STORY-998** — Information-based idle detection (idlewatch) over agent event streams: a Spinning watchdog trip for drains and a spinning liveness state for sessions (#1768)
- **STORY-1047** — aida schedule: a native periodic-task registry that runs due maintenance sweeps opportunistically (no daemon), plus emit-cron for the rest (#1767)
- **TASK-1211** — aida mailbox: retire read/stale messages so the inbox read-view is not buried under OBE handoffs (archive + unread-default) (#1742)

- **SPEC-442** — History strftime templates reject decoded event timestamps
- **SPIKE-87** — Benchmark TypeSafe AI's Jev against historical review verdicts and store contradictions
- **STORY-1050** — Queue-depth backpressure: relax the REVIEWER (not build/test) for low-risk specs under load, with optimistic-merge + auto-revert on red CI
- **STORY-1130** — aida drain start + stop: complete the drain-noun lifecycle as a thin front-door over the existing engine
- **STORY-1163** — GitLab Slice 0: wire the forge dispatcher into the central CI/PR helpers (GitHub-neutral)
- **STORY-1164** — GitLab Slice 1: route the high-traffic keyboard paths through the Forge trait
- **STORY-1173** — Proxy-approval ledger: comment-anchored, marked, separately surfaceable
- **STORY-1221** — Typed protocols: per-requirement-type (and per-lane) protocol stored as META, injected at pickup and per turn while the lease is held, and checked at done
- **STORY-1226** — aida cron: per-seat periodic job registry + ledger (substrate jobs run by the scheduler tick, seat jobs delivered to whoever holds the role, vendor-neutral)
- **STORY-1350** — Implementer posts an approach statement before its first edit (criteria-as-tests with the layer named, files to touch, ambiguities as questions); opt-in advisor preview for drive/guided
- **STORY-1351** — Seat handoff pack: make product/advisor reliability survive a cold session by moving each learned behaviour into a gate, a scheduled job or a role file — not memory
- **STORY-1353** — Dispatch authority is not advisor authority: let the proxy seat route work in its own right, keep disposition with the advisor and integrity floors with the human
- **STORY-1422** — The usage query exits 0 whether it finds ten calls over budget or none, so BUG-1480's latency guard has no failure mode - give it one and trigger it from a substrate job
- **STORY-1424** — Let an acceptance criterion carry an executable check the reviewer RUNS instead of reads — graded, never a hard rule
- **STORY-1426** — Nothing detects a semantic contradiction in the store — every reference in the VIS-1 / CR-6 conflict resolves, and it stood for three months
- **STORY-1473** — Seat authority is session-bound and set at a TTY; the roster caps which seats a user may take; roster writes are human-at-TTY only
- **STORY-1477** — aida history --template/--fields with named, scoped, saveable templates (user/project/builtin)
- **STORY-1478** — Per-spec timeline: aida history <SPEC> --timeline (work/wait/unknown spans across store, events, PR/CI)
- **STORY-1655** — Audited per-project solo-human review mode: a TTY-verified human may approve their own claim when [review] solo_human = true
- **STORY-1664** — aida history opened|completed|rejected|approved|closed: list-shaped views of what changed in a time window
- **STORY-1673** — Constraint rows carry a verify command and expected value; aida verify constraints flags host drift
- **STORY-1674** — Record model, harness version, effort and session tokens on every spec write (filed_at and edits)
- **STORY-1675** — Hypothesis rows for pre-registration: prediction, refutation criterion, linked experiments, locked once a result is linked
- **STORY-1676** — aida regen score: install via the system's own installer, flag crash-dominated scores, and audit oracle assumptions with cross-family runs
- **STORY-1677** — aida regen trial: run-until-done policy and a completeness check, so effort is not scored as spec quality
- **STORY-1679** — Cross-model consensus failure as an automatic store-gap detector
- **STORY-1687** — A Rework merge-hold releases itself on a fresh independent APPROVE at the current head, so approved PRs do not wait on operator presence
- **STORY-1693** — aida session handoff --show prefixes standing orders, so a fresh seat never loses durable operator rulings
- **STORY-1697** — Auto-release a review-placed merge hold when an attested approval is recorded at the PR's current head
- **STORY-1698** — Fire-fighting mandate: a human-minted, time-boxed grant that lets the advisor seat ship held PRs with attested independent approval
- **STORY-1699** — Fleet digest and heartbeat: an hourly compact 'what's in progress' report with automatic stall/blocked escalation
- **STORY-1854** — Callers hand cargo time budgets to the wrapper (AIDA_CARGO_TIMEOUT) instead of an outer timeout
- **TASK-1232** — aida agent new --noexec/--print-command: show the resolved command line + permission posture (sandbox/approval) without launching
- **TASK-1233** — aida init / scaffold: detect missing-or-incoherent permission posture and offer to set contained (extend TASK-698 first-machine agent-config)
- **TASK-1244** — Merge-lease: extend the lease to the drain merge phase (serialize drain-vs-pr-ship)
- **TASK-1246** — [AFK] Slice 1 (tracer bullet): criteria IDs + trace:SPEC.ACn on tests + aida criteria gap report
- **TASK-1247** — [HITL] Slice 2: aida harvest <spec> [--diff] — standalone harvest gate (agent + selectivity filter + confirm-checklist + [aida:harvest] ledger)
- **TASK-1250** — aida mailbox send: accept body via --body-file/--stdin (avoid shell-substitution trap)
- **TASK-1254** — Live GitLab drain validation: headless --auto-complete on the self-hosted mirror once glab is authenticated and the project has a pipeline
- **TASK-1272** — aida drain resume <SPEC>: re-drive parked work from the phase that failed, reusing an already-green pipeline
- **TASK-1285** — Version pinning + rebuild handoff for a running wave: the drain records the binary SHA it started on, refuses a mid-wave binary swap, and hands off to a rebuilt binary at a phase boundary
- **TASK-1288** — aida merge-hold add <pr>: place a hand-hold symmetrically (marker + label) so a PR held outside a drain releases through aida merge-hold clear
- **TASK-1290** — Wire the existing aida criteria trace into the done-gate and the reviewer prompt: an untraced acceptance criterion is named before review, not discovered by it
- **TASK-1296** — A Review-PR spec resolves when its PR reaches a terminal state: merged closes it Completed, closed-unmerged closes it Rejected — today six sat Approved against dead PRs and inflated the queue depth us
- **TASK-1297** — A --batch drain must report what it SKIPPED, not only what it drained: the launcher drove a batch of three while sixteen approved specs routed to the same role sat invisible for hours
- **TASK-1298** — The pre-unattended readiness check covers the substrate but not the machine, and passed forty minutes before the disk hit 100 percent
- **TASK-1427** — Build a derived per-message token ledger attributable to spec, run, phase, round, seat and vendor
- **TASK-1434** — Add a dated rate table and auditable per-spec cost query over the token ledger
- **TASK-1499** — Make aida scaffold portable across local binary installations
- **TASK-1517** — Per-wave resource limits for systemd-run drain waves (MemoryHigh/MemoryMax, CPU/IO weights, TasksMax, delegation doctor warning)
- **TASK-1520** — Dev repo: make sync-templates/check-templates manage .agents/skills as regular-file copies with a drift check, plus the remaining docs
- **TASK-1542** — Drain stop follow-ups: forward SIGTERM to in-flight vendor children on a manual stop, and record 'stopped' in the drain-state file
- **TASK-1587** — Allow aida list to be sorted by id
- **TASK-1589** — Add CI status column to aida pr list output
- **TASK-1597** — aida role enter: prompt for sub-launch seat delegation at TTY entry (ADR-66 UX follow-up)
- **TASK-1607** — STORY-1485 slice A: graceful single-occupancy path (--kill fails closed as scaffolding)
- **TASK-1614** — Add 'faq' requirement type
- **TASK-1729** — aida regen corpus: freeze the store export into a deterministic corpus dir
- **TASK-1730** — Project regen config and aida regen score (R1): run the declared oracle in a sandbox and record the score
- **TASK-1731** — Regen controls gate: reference, stub and clean-sandbox controls must pass before a trial score is reported
- **TASK-1734** — Surface the regenerability score and its age in aida statusline and aida digest
- **TASK-1737** — Record the authorizing seat and grant on advisor-gated status advances (commit trailers)
- **TASK-1744** — Deliver mid-session grant expiry notices to Codex and Antigravity tool-only sessions
- **TASK-1780** — Tier local testing by risk: fixer/relay templates stop running the full suite per PR; nightly full-suite job
- **TASK-1783** — Report whole-tree Rust function trace coverage
- **TASK-1785** — Report unexplained Rust numeric constants in traced code
- **TASK-1787** — Operator-chosen grant TTL: 'aida role enter <seat> --ttl <dur>' (human TTY only), capped by [seat] max_grant_hours (default 24, hard ceiling 168h)
- **TASK-1790** — STORY-1698 follow-ups: exclusions ledger perms/owner check, require_direct_tty wording, operator-only exclusion beyond named IDs, re-arm audit ordering
- **TASK-190** — Shell command-substitution has silently eaten text out of at least two spec descriptions, and the holes have a signature that finds them
- **TASK-1960** — STORY-1690 S1: patch-id attestation carry-over — a merge-from-main that leaves the PR's own diff unchanged keeps its attested approval (fixes BUG-2177)

### Fixes

- **BUG-2181** — Remote calls outside the mirror path still put an operator-embedded HTTP token in git-remote-http argv (#2774)
- **BUG-2118** — Identify and reproduce the history fixture Git temporary-file/object-write failure (#2771)
- **TASK-1791** — regen score: bound each generated-tool run (--help completeness probe, installer, oracle) with a timeout recorded as an incomplete/failed reason (#2770)
- **TASK-1968** — Sweep: verdict-reader and approval carry-over polish (PR-2712 / PR-2758 reviews) (#2768)
- **BUG-2140** — Contained store writes have no automatic host commit: session end doesn't sweep pending store changes under default sync config (#2749)
- **TASK-1792** — relay-stall check: detect SIGKILL/OOM-dead relays still marked running, ignore run-dir handoff.md.done as an ack, and age-cap or distinguish deliberate Ctrl-C exits (#2701)
- **TASK-1784** — worktree add after a hand-deleted worktree refuses with a misleading 'no local lease holds it' instead of re-minting (#2698)
- **BUG-2164** — aida pr ship with no PR number on the default branch pushes main and tries 'gh pr create main->main' instead of refusing (#2697)
- **BUG-2170** — Autopilot intake and questions-clarify children do not scrub AIDA_SESSION_PURPOSE, and the intake child grant is not revoked on exit (#2696)
- **BUG-2171** — Contained parent escape via burndown run, autopilot intake and questions clarify: BUG-2124 builders pass containment_applied(Claude, false), so they always strip AIDA_CONTAINED and default to bypassPe (#2696)
- **BUG-2166** — Contained parent escape: 'aida queue work --plan-only --permission-mode bypassPermissions' launches an uncontained bypass claude with no consent gate (#2693)
- **BUG-2139** — A fresh headless queue-work child inherits the launcher's full grant (including delegable seats), so an advisor running implementer work headless does it with advisor authority (#2688)
- **TASK-1782** — agent stop: distinguish recycled vs unknown identity in the not-signalled message; test a live process with pid_start=None (#2681)
- **BUG-2148** — aida agent stop and session end's leaked-process kill signal by pid-alive only, with no start-time identity check (pid-reuse kill risk) (#2676)
- **TASK-1775** — BUG-2131 hardening: write the contained-store marker before the git version check; route git_backend's raw diff --cached through host_git_command (#2670)
- **TASK-1769** — Schedule driver follow-ups from #2654: canonicalize live-path compare; document invalid-job nonzero tick; de-host shift install test (#2669)
- **BUG-2133** — Contained drain launches its phase_driver CI-fix and advisor child sessions uncontained (#2655)
- **BUG-2142** — Scheduler tick unit pins the install-time binary (target/release/aida); one unknown job command then aborts the whole tick, silently stopping every substrate job (#2654)
- **BUG-2141** — ensure_aida_runtime_excluded writes an unanchored '.aida/' into the shared info/exclude, silently ignoring tracked .aida/discipline and template masters in every worktree (#2652)
- **BUG-2132** — Mailbox accepts junk recipient ids: ~8h ago a sender's word-split args created mailboxes like '-include', '33492', 'BUG-52', 'L4425', 'SPEC', each holding ~18 unread (5 urgent) messages (#2650)
- **BUG-2134** — A merged PR whose trailer names a needs-attention spec does not complete it, so the spec stays needs-attention after its work ships (#2649)
- **BUG-2124** — Uncontained launchers (phase_driver, burndown, autopilot intake, questions, compete) do not strip inherited AIDA_CONTAINED (#2646)
- **BUG-1991** — Session end skips every live-process holder check when the session's own MCP claim shares the worktree path (#2631)
- **BUG-2127** — Flake: cached_git_backend reader_ancestor_check_timeout_is_counted_and_never_writes fails on CI (#2623)
- **BUG-1994** — Tests that prepend a fake git to the process-wide PATH leak into concurrent tests (history.rs git calls get fake git / exit 127) (#2597, #2618)
- **BUG-2121** — verify-manual.py completeness counts PATH extensions (aida-<name> executables) as commands, so every local drain publication is auto-closed on a machine with aida-* plugins (#2616)
- **BUG-2120** — Runaway-seat watchdog reports a dead session's never-deleted cron as an active mail poll forever, so the night shift's watchdog-quiet guard refuses every wave (#2613)
- **BUG-2006** — Contained launch paths other than queue work never export AIDA_CONTAINED=1, so contained-aware checks treat those sessions as uncontained (#2610)
- **BUG-2013** — Markdown terminal renderer: no test that the computed glyph profile reaches render (#2607)
- **BUG-2008** — Pre-commit /// gate still refuses a wrapped prose sentence whose spec ID lands alone on the next line (BUG-629 residue) (#2605)
- **BUG-2011** — Grant-expiry lookup validates the grant separately from grant_check, so role expiry can pass a grant the authority gates reject (#2604)
- **BUG-2004** — GitLab release probe passes PRIVATE-TOKEN to curl on the command line (visible in ps) (#2599)
- **BUG-1962** — runaway-seats lists sessions that have ended or gone idle as still_active compaction findings (#2598)
- **BUG-2002** — agent new --spec: lease conflict is reported only after the interactive prompts, and session leases hides the blocking lease (#2596)
- **BUG-1999** — aida dev recovery misattributes prev's record when an old-format .rollback/.promoted leftover is present (#2595)

- **BUG-1298** — A CI-red shelve can name the wrong workflow run in its recovery hint, sending the implementer to a run that contains no failure
- **BUG-1299** — A watchdog retry reaches the operator as the bare word watchdog: SpecRetried has no detail field, so a stalled session and a productive one that ran out of clock are indistinguishable
- **BUG-1310** — Classifiers that read external tool prose can drift with no edit of ours and no test failing, and were scoped out of the exit-code sweep
- **BUG-1418** — The drain exit record reports zero tokens for 57 of 92 drains, indistinguishably from a genuine zero, so it cannot be used as a cost ledger
- **BUG-1420** — The implementer preflight runs CI's guards under a more permissive shell than CI does, so a guard can pass locally and fail in CI
- **BUG-1429** — The in-drive ship guard decides liveness by matching a state string another module formats, so a reword opens the gate
- **BUG-1441** — aida drain status shows an empty in-progress view while actively working a spec, because current and members disagree
- **BUG-1442** — aida show in TOON collapses every relationship type to Related and drops the Blockers section, so agents cannot see a spec is blocked
- **BUG-1454** — The Done to Completed auto-bump fires on the first trailered commit, so a multi-deliverable spec is marked Completed with unmerged work in an open PR that no open-work surface will ever show again
- **BUG-1461** — MCP send_message cannot set a mail subject after BUG-1231 added --subject to the CLI
- **BUG-1464** — Starter role system_prompt never reaches an already-initialized machine - neither aida init nor scaffold refresh backfills it
- **BUG-1465** — Mailbox CLI print path lost two checks the core projection has: an empty --subject renders a blank bold line, and an ambiguous --in-reply-to prefix reports not-found
- **BUG-1466** — aida review record --pr writes a PR handshake with no provenance: reviewed_sha, reviewed_branch, recorded_at and recorded_by are all dropped (452 of 481 files affected)
- **BUG-1471** — aida rel add --type related succeeds with a note and produces an edge no graph traversal follows, and related is the most natural word to reach for
- **BUG-1485** — The orchestrator cannot match its own child session to a lease — four occurrences, three today, and one of them stranded pushed work as a done spec with no PR
- **BUG-1487** — aida queue move is the only queue verb without --user, so the documented way to reorder another identity's queue fails — and it is a write, so the caller silently reorders the wrong one
- **BUG-1494** — The NeedsAttention to Approved advisor gate is enforced on aida edit but not on queue rework, so the documented recovery sequence walks around it in two hops
- **BUG-1498** — The advisor's draft queue is 26 of 39 machine-filed shelve records presented as if a person filed them, so grooming pays a judgment per machine row
- **BUG-1502** — aida show --format json is silently ignored, the third instance of a class whose generalising criterion shipped unmet with BUG-1289
- **BUG-1511** — Eight personal dotfiles sit untracked and UNIGNORED at the root of a public repo, so any git add -A publishes whatever later fills them
- **BUG-1516** — A verdict's recorded sha is sometimes abbreviated, so a gate comparing it to the PR head by string equality reports stale for one in five current verdicts
- **BUG-1518** — A tree-diff against a behind-branch shows deletions the merge will never make, so a reviewer refuses a PR for a revert that cannot happen
- **BUG-1520** — aida list --fields is silently ignored AND unvalidated under --format json, and the JSON projection spells the type key req_type while every other surface spells it type
- **BUG-1538** — Review verdicts are written without a reviewed_sha two times in three even in the current era, so a verdict that cannot be checked for staleness reads as an approval
- **BUG-1543** — TASK-1296's stranded Review-PR sweep skips Draft, so a review story left Draft by a failed queueing step is never resolved against its terminal PR
- **BUG-1545** — short_sha_for_row carries pluralize's doc line on main, so a sha truncator is documented as a count formatter and pluralize is undocumented
- **BUG-1546** — Compare a verdict sha by commit identity with a minimum-length floor, and report cannot-determine distinctly from stale (BUG-1516 criteria 1 and 4)
- **BUG-1548** — A drain runs every four minutes and aida drain status, schedule list, cron, systemd and an anchored pgrep all report nothing
- **BUG-1550** — Three doc/trace headers were orphaned by insertion in one night and all compiled clean - gate the class, because a displaced trace comment silently inverts aida why
- **BUG-1554** — A new aida doctor category must be hand-registered in three places and the compiler checks none of them - the user-facing valid-name list has already drifted by two categories
- **BUG-1557** — A scaffolded [[schedule.jobs]] example whose command is not in parse_scheduled_command's closed allowlist errors at every tick forever, and nothing checks the two hand-maintained lists against each ot
- **BUG-1558** — aida show --json carries no relationships field at all - and the wider 'every type renders as Related' claim was measured on a stale binary, not the source
- **BUG-1559** — The compile-time guard's witness test asserts the impl exists, not that the show path is typed against it - widening the signature back to a concrete backend passes green
- **BUG-1560** — The stale-review-flip sweep excludes Draft at three sites for the same unexamined reason BUG-1543 just fixed in its sibling - the exclusion is by omission, not by judgement
- **BUG-1567** — awaiting_notice_tracks_real_lease_through_session_end is load-correlated flaky on main, and it is what failed the GitLab pipeline that TASK-1274 is being judged by
- **BUG-1569** — awaiting --notice's protocol-line lookup does a full backend.load(), racing its own 1s-derived fail-open watchdog
- **BUG-1570** — A drain-lock collision is reported as a phase-2 failure of the first batch member, naming an innocent spec and telling the reader to fix it
- **BUG-1572** — The performance gate counts every binary's calls in one machine-global log, so a stale worktree build can hold main's gate red for a regression that is not on main
- **BUG-1573** — A performance-guard trip ledgers result: failed:1 and nothing else - the proportion, denominator, budget and tolerance in force at the time of the trip are discarded, so the record cannot tell a fixed
- **BUG-1575** — The blank-subject predicate re-duplicated at a fourth site three hours after the merge that removed a duplicate of it, and the new copy documents the duplication instead of removing it
- **BUG-1576** — aida awaiting reports squash-merged branches as unshipped work and tells the reader to ship them, because every detection path it could use compares commit content that squashing destroys
- **BUG-1579** — starter_role_refresh_adds_missing_prompt_and_preserves_user_prompt is nondeterministic and reds a required check on unrelated PRs — proven by a rerun at the same sha
- **BUG-1581** — Two reviewers recorded opposite verdicts on the same commit into two different artefacts and nothing reconciles them, so whether a PR is approved depends on which file the reader opens
- **BUG-1582** — The doc-intent gate resolves specs by filename (spec_id) while every human-facing surface uses agreed_id, so 96 specs are unresolvable to a required check and their doc-impact tags are silently unenfo
- **BUG-1584** — The spec card collapses BlockedBy, Blocks, Duplicate and Verifies into one Related heading, so it cannot say a spec is blocked by something — the edge names it already carries are discarded
- **BUG-1585** — docs/autonomous-drain.md says pipeline_depth defaults to 2 and the code says 1, and the PR that checked it reported clean from a grep for a different phrasing
- **BUG-1586** — The --sequential drain notice prints the DEFAULT pipeline_depth instead of the resolved one, so in this repo it announces strictly-one-at-a-time and then pipelines at depth 2

- **BUG-1588** — A review test created fixture requirements in the live canonical store
- **BUG-1656** — aida ps/awaiting flag Agent-tool subagents' live spec work as orphaned/abandoned and suggest a salvage-commit into the worktree they are editing
- **BUG-1657** — aida session reap sees 'no merge signal' for specs landed through batched integration PRs, so finished sessions never reap
- **BUG-1661** — Pre-commit hook and docs polish: rg -nE in user-facing-text docs (-E is --encoding), hook treats //// as a doc comment, cargo-fmt step claims 'drift fixed' when cargo itself fails
- **BUG-1662** — aida scaffold refresh on a memory-lane project floods it: adds the 26-file discipline pack and replaces the lane AGENTS.md block with the full AIDA Conventions block
- **BUG-1671** — Archive and queue sweeps re-check a spec outside the store lock, so a reopen between the re-check and the write is overwritten
- **BUG-1672** — aida human lists specs under reviews-awaiting after an independent review has already approved them
- **BUG-1675** — The disk-headroom guard runs the full doctor store load for a single statvfs, taking 44 s under load and timing out at 120 s into a false CronJobFailed disk trip
- **BUG-1676** — GitLab mirror hub drifts for hours: aida remote mirror-push finishes in ~11 ms and appears not to push
- **BUG-1678** — get_requirement_targeted falls back to a full-store parse on a cache miss, so 'aida show --json' can scan the whole store once per relationship target
- **BUG-1682** — Global role queue (~/.aida/queue/<role>.yaml) is a read-modify-write with a bare fs::write and no lock, so concurrent writers drop entries
- **BUG-1683** — A live drain older than 30 minutes can have its lock reclaimed by queue work --auto-complete / burndown run (decide_lock age backstop never refreshed)
- **BUG-1684** — make build-fast works with a configured sccache wrapper
- **BUG-1685** — Doctor scaffold drift detects skills on Windows
- **BUG-1689** — Fifth ETXTBSY fixture-exec failure: production retries it, test fixtures do not, and each test file rolls its own fake_gh
- **BUG-1691** — write_typed_hold overwrites an existing hold, so a Recusal hold can be silently replaced by a Rework hold
- **BUG-1713** — auto-complete failure: phase 2 (ci) on BUG-1689

- **BUG-1720** — Bypass consent gate does not detect --permission-mode=bypassPermissions, so the equals form is never confirmed

### Fixes

- **BUG-1063** — Headless phases die waiting: sessions either end their turn expecting a background notification that never arrives under claude -p, or block in one long gh run watch that emits nothing until it return (#1748)
- **TASK-153** — aida ps still shows lease role for a long-lived advisor whose transcript conversations calls advisor (#1741)
- **BUG-909** — Watchdog is blind to a working Codex implementer: codex exec prints nothing to stdout until it finishes, so 'no session output for 10m' kills every Codex phase that thinks for 10 minutes (#1740)
- **BUG-908** — STORY-975 retry cannot reclaim the lease attempt 1 left behind — attempt 2 dies in seconds with 'scope is owned by lease … worktree DIRTY' (#1739)
- **BUG-1021** — cross-platform nightly red on macOS two consecutive nights (main d6cdad0 and 18e7ee5) (#1738)
- **BUG-912** — review record --pr writes the phase-3 handshake under AIDA_DRIVE_ROOT (the worktree) and ignores AIDA_REVIEW_VERDICT_FILE, so a headless verdict lands where phase 4 never looks (#1737)
- **BUG-1056** — queue rework leaves the spec NeedsAttention, which the unified pickability policy refuses — reworks became undrainable (#1736)
- **TASK-154** — git push hook calls missing aida remote mirror-push (#1735)
- **BUG-1052** — integrate --run: propagate headless mode to member re-drives and optionally wait on in-flight CI (#1731)
- **BUG-1044** — aida status leads with a no-active-role banner after reboot — last-used role and a copyable re-enter command (#1727)
- **BUG-1043** — MCP approval authority can disagree with the active shell role — refusals must name both roles and the relaunch command (#1726)
- **BUG-1038** — reviewer phase-child hits the queue-membership identity refusal — phase children need the pipeline's queue user, not just the phase role (#1725)
- **BUG-1037** — reviewer preflight checks resolve the forge as pure-git on a github-origin project — one forge resolution for the whole pipeline (#1724)
- **TASK-1202** — retire PROMPT_HISTORY from AIDA-scaffolded discipline — substrate and digest are the session record (#1723)
- **BUG-1025** — aida pull autostash can leave conflict markers inside .aida/config.toml — silently corrupting config for every later command (#1721)
- **BUG-1020** — aida agent type picker lists disabled vendors — the enabled-agents profile must gate the picker (#1718)
- **BUG-1019** — duplicate-agent guard offers Launch another anyway, then refuses anyway — the yes path must work or the offer must not exist (#1714)
- **BUG-1018** — Draft status glyph is the oversized circle — use the small circle to match the status column (#1713)
- **BUG-1017** — unified queue pickability policy — Done entries selectable by next/dry-run, NeedsAttention accepted without triage or force (#1711)
- **BUG-916** — submodule init on worktree creation ineffective in the field — empty gitlinks recur on 0.14.0 despite the BUG-899 fix (#1710)
- **BUG-915** — worktree .git file breaks git inside dev containers — gitdir points at a host path the container cannot see (#1709)
- **BUG-914** — worktrees expose .aida/ and .aida-store as untracked files — stage-into-MR hazard; use worktree-local git excludes (#1708)
- **BUG-913** — no-launch and dry-run paths bypass vendor resolution — disabled claude displayed and completion instructions hardcode claude (#1707)
- **BUG-906** — phase retry collides with its own dead predecessor's lease — retry must reap a lease whose process exited (#1702)
- **BUG-902** — BUG-898 vendor preflight is headless-gated — interactive queue work still resolves disabled claude and mutates state first (#1701)
- **BUG-901** — role-routed queue entries must be pickable by the orchestrator regardless of the adder's user identity — BUG-900 over-rotation (#1700)
- **BUG-900** — queue routing fractures across user identities — advisor requeue succeeds into a queue the implementer never sees (#1699)
- **BUG-899** — AIDA-created worktrees leave recursive submodules uninitialized — builds fail on empty gitlinks (#1698)
- **BUG-898** — queue work launches the disabled claude vendor instead of the enabled codex — after already creating the worktree (#1697)
- **BUG-896** — auto-open resolves the forge-less provider in the phase-3 recovery context — no-op open_change returns id 0 as success (#1695)
- **BUG-895** — phase-3 no-pr preflight must invoke the BUG-893 auto-open using the pushed branch after the worktree is gone (#1694)
- **BUG-893** — orchestrator opens the PR itself when the branch is pushed but no PR exists (#1693)
- **BUG-890** — interactive human review refuses on a stale lease instead of offering release-and-proceed (#1691)
- **BUG-888** — node-qualified spec ids (TASK-1-127) are mangled to a different id (TASK-151) by resolvers and branch/trailer derivation (#1690)
- **STORY-974** — Every drain shelve names a typed cause that a stranger can read (#1687)
- **BUG-882** — reviewer-scoped queue work on a Done spec is refused by the implementer preflight (#1686)
- **BUG-881** — from-pr resolver misses open PRs and must share the forge-first resolution (#1685)
- **BUG-880** — tail drain resolves a finished pipeline as live and exits silently on plain-text logs (#1684)
- **BUG-879** — phase 3 fabricates PR-0 when the implementer never opened a PR — reviewer launches against a nonexistent PR (#1683)
- **BUG-878** — phase-2 session teardown proceeds with unpushed implementer commits — CI and review run on the stale head (#1682)
- **BUG-876** — human review loses the spec's open PR once the lease is released — claims 'never pushed' despite trailered commit and open PR (#1680)
- **BUG-875** — reviewer-phase watchdog keys on commits/file-changes — kills every review longer than the window (#1678)
- **BUG-872** — pacing last-output age resolves the previous attempt's log — current session id must win (#1677)
- **BUG-862** — queue drain drives reviewer-routed items with implementer sessions — degenerate 10m watchdog kills (#1676)
- **BUG-868** — from-pr reviewer session reviews nothing when the spec is already Done — does queue cleanup instead of reviewing the PR (#1675)
- **BUG-866** — drain status pacing anchors spec/phase elapsed to drain start, not spec start (#1674)
- **BUG-864** — auto-filed phase-failure drafts do not dedupe — one doomed retry loop filed 7 duplicate records (#1673)
- **BUG-852** — drain exit summary double-counts retried specs and exits 0 despite shelves (#1669)
- **BUG-816** — human review open-PR hint emits gh pr create --fill, which fails from main — must pass --head <branch> (#1668)
- **BUG-836** — remote status labels a pure fast-forward lag as DIVERGED — should say BEHIND and suggest a plain push (#1667)
- **BUG-853** — session-end 'unshipped commits for PR-N with no open PR' warning false-positives on open and merged PRs (#1666)
- **BUG-851** — queue rework re-queues with empty for_role at the queue tail — unroutable and unordered (#1664)
- **STORY-835** — init's antigravity profile actually wires agy: AGENTS.md reach check + one-time --add-mcp offer + doctor probe (#1661)
- **BUG-842** — tail drain resolves a weeks-stale burndown log and exits — the live queue-work drain is invisible to it (#1660)
- **TASK-1194** — aida status leads with a live-drain line when a drain is in flight (#1658)
- **TASK-1193** — docs/plans/ is created on first use, not at init (#1656)
- **BUG-840** — role enter full-store load makes entering a role take 30s — route the title lookup through the cache (#1652)
- **BUG-839** — queue work --drain ignores --dry-run and launches a real drain (#1650)
- **BUG-838** — init silently skips a pre-existing AGENTS.md — AIDA-AUTOGEN block is never injected (#1648)
- **BUG-837** — session list role column misses AIDA hook-format role markers so known-role sessions show '-' (#1646)
- **BUG-826** — phase-1 launch resilience: retry an instant vendor death with backoff; auto-release the lease a zero-output session leaves behind (#1645)
- **TASK-1192** — auto-filed phase-failure drafts should auto-resolve when their spec completes (#1642)
- **TASK-1189** — review verdict findings[] has a consumer but no producer (#1641)

- **TASK-150** — Codex pickup prompt references unavailable aida-pickup skill (#1629)
- **BUG-807** — flaky test: mailbox publish_for_sync_stages_canonical fails intermittently even in near-isolation (#1628)
- **BUG-795** — queue work --auto-complete: items routed --for implementer are un-drainable and every error hides the way out (#1627)

- **BUG-809** — phase-3 verdict handshake: env anchor does not survive vendor tool sandbox — anchor via prompt text + sibling-checkout sweep (#1620)

- **BUG-802** — reviewer verdict lands in the wrong directory: teach the existing "aida review verdict" verb the drive-root anchor and put it in the prompt (#1614)
- **BUG-799** — codex exec does not expand /aida-* prompts — drain phases send literal slash-commands; inline-render at the launch boundary (#1613)
